New PURE `needs_ref_text(entry)` (tts-mlx + ref_audio + no ref_text) is checked in `/api/voice/tts`, and `_heal_ref_text` transcribes once and PERSISTS it through `_registry_update` before the render that would otherwise pay for it. Pin-time and render-time now share ONE implementation (`_transcribe_clip`, asserted by test to be called from exactly two places). **HONEST NOTE: OmniVoice generation itself is legitimately 5-15s for a paragraph** (iterative unmask over N steps) — the cache is what makes REPEATS instant; first-render cost is the model's nature, not a bug left unfixed. **PART 2 — LIBRARY UX.** (2a) **`⊕ add clip file`** chip → hidden `<input type=file accept="audio/*">` (`runOpenPanelWith` is already implemented in the Swift shell — without it the dialog is the same silent no-op that broke ⊕ attach) → non-wav is re-encoded client-side through the EXISTING `wavFromBuffer` (same reason as the recorder: the engine's m4a path needs ffprobe and our provisioned ffmpeg ships none) → saved into the library → auto-pinned. (2b) **Folder sources**: `GET/POST /api/voice/library/folders[/add|/remove]` + `GET /api/voice/library?folder=`. A folder is a **POINTER, never a copy** — nothing is imported, removing one forgets the pointer and the endpoint contains no delete at all (test-asserted). Listing is NON-recursive, capped at 200, and **`folder_allowed` is the entire boundary**: an EXACT-match allowlist of folders the user themselves added — a CHILD of a configured folder is refused (no prefix matching), because this endpoint reads a directory named by a query parameter. Folder clips are pinned by ABSOLUTE PATH (entry-ref already accepts one; the panel routes on a leading '/', which a library basename can never have). Suggested folders are **EXISTENCE-GATED** (a one-click offer that leads to an error is worse than no offer). (2c) **Clear/unpin + HIDE**: a faint `clear` beside the Voice row label on both the named-voice and the clip picker (`clearPinLink`, shared); new `POST /api/models/hide` writes `hidden:true` through `_registry_update` for **read-only imports ONLY** (`HIDEABLE_SOURCES` = lmstudio-import / jan-import / audio-hf-cache — an app-owned row already has a real Delete, and one row must never have two ways to vanish), refuses a model that is currently the runner / aux / default TTS / default STT, and **the filtering happens at the BRIDGE, not the panel** (`installed`, `audio` AND `/api/voice/config`'s `available`), so a hidden model cannot leak into the chat popover through a renderer that forgot to filter; a `N hidden — show` toggle at each list bottom unhides. (2d) **tts sub-badges** `voices` / `cloning` on Audio rows via PURE `audioVoiceBadge` (declared names WIN over the cloning verdict, same rule as the picker; no verdict ⇒ no badge), zero new CSS. **REAL BUG FOUND WHILE BUILDING: `seed_registry.merge()` would have un-pinned every REFERENCE CLIP and un-hidden everything on one RESCAN** — the shipped `_keep_voice` carry-forward covered only `voice`, and ref_audio / ref_text / hidden are the same class of user decision that lives nowhere on disk. New `USER_KEYS` + `_keep_user` carries all four (and lmstudio imports now get it too, which they never did). Tests: new **`test_voice_cache.py` (90 checks:** key decision table incl. the stamp + boundary-forging cases, LRU-vs-insertion-order eviction, the byte budget, oversized-payload refusal, the self-heal table, folder containment/cap/non-recursion/corrupt-file tables, suggestion gating, wiring greps), test_audio_registry.py +9 (ref/transcript/hidden survive a rescan), test_audio_rows.js +15 (badge + hideable tables, panel-vs-bridge allowlist byte-match). **All 21 py + 11 js suites green, contract 57, CSS 491/491 UNCHANGED, ast OK, both panel JS blocks node --check clean.** **⚠️ PENDING FABLE QA:** (1) the folder LIST is persisted in `data/voice_folders.json`, **not harness.yaml** — `_set_yaml_scalar` is a line-scan SCALAR writer and teaching the critical ops path a list shape (which ship.sh's manifest merge also round-trips) is not a builder's call; a folder list is per-machine runtime state exactly like data/models.json; (2) the VoiceStudio suggested-folder paths are **UNVERIFIED** (the component is not cloned in the sandbox) — derived from what start_component.sh does know (cwd=vendor/voicestudio, voicebox's explicit --data-dir) and existence-gated so a wrong guess costs nothing; (3) `⊕ add folder` is a TYPED PATH box, not a directory picker (WKWebView folder pickers are the ⊕-attach class of silent no-op); (4) hiding is refused for a model in use rather than silently clearing the role; (5) the cache is process-lifetime with no TTL and survives an Unload (the bytes stay valid — only the RAM claim went away); (6) two clips with the same BASENAME in different folders both render as "pinned" in the picker (`ref_audio` is exposed as a basename by design, so the path never reaches the panel); (7) `_keep_user` lets a freshly scanned value WIN over a remembered one (nothing scans these keys today, but the rule is "the scan wins when the scan knows"); (8) an added clip file whose container the browser cannot decode is stored raw and 400s if its suffix is outside wav/mp3/flac/m4a. **UNVERIFIED until the Mac:** real second-listen latency (the cache is exercised only by unit tests — mlx cannot run in the sandbox), whether WKWebView's `decodeAudioData` handles arbitrary picked files, and the actual on-disk layout of VoiceStudio's output dir. **Debi verify:** ▶ speak a reply → the bridge log prints one `[voice] render …s` line with the breakdown → ▶ speak the SAME reply again → **instant**, log says `render CACHED`; Models → Audio → a cloning model → `⊕ add clip file` picks a wav and pins it; `folders ⌄` → paste a path → `⊕ add folder` → the folder chip expands into pinnable clips; an LM-Studio / HF-cache row → `hide from list` (two-step) → it disappears from BOTH the Models list and the composer picker → `N hidden — show` → unhide → RESCAN → the pinned clip, its transcript and the hidden flag all survive. Ship: `./scripts/ship.sh`.

> **🔧 30s-RENDER ROOT-CAUSED + SPEAK MARKDOWN FIX (2026-08-13, Fable-built):** Debi: every OmniVoice render took ~30s even re-listens, and ▶ speak read "asterisk asterisk" on bold text. (1) **Root cause (venv source, generate.py): a pinned clip with NO ref_text makes generate_audio load whisper-large-v3-turbo (~1.6GB) to transcribe the clip on EVERY render, then discard it** ("Ref_text not found. Transcribing ref_audio…"). FIX: `/api/voice/entry-ref` now AUTO-TRANSCRIBES the clip ONCE at pin time with the harness's own default STT (whisper-base, sub-second per Gate 2) and stores ref_text (≤500) on the entry; best-effort (no STT default / failure ⇒ empty = old slow-but-working path). The resident worker then renders without any whisper load. (2) **speakText now strips markdown emphasis/headers/bullets** (**bold**/*em*/__/_/#/-) — bounded regexes, lone `*` in prose survives; +6 cases in test_speak_text.js. (3) Clone quality CONFIRMED by Debi (weather read in the cloned voice). **QUEUED (Fable→builder next): "⊕ add clip file" picker chip** (entry-ref already accepts any path; VoiceStudio-generated voices reach the library as exported files — true system-audio capture rejected: needs a loopback driver); **tts sub-badges `voices|cloning`** on Audio rows (audio_entry_view already carries `cloning`); **curated named-voice model starters** (research: good-quality TTS that SHIPS voices, per Debi "shouldn't have to clone"). All suites green.

> **✅ FABLE QA: REF-AUDIO SLICE APPROVED + GATE 2 MEASURED (2026-08-13, Fable 5):** all 7 builder tags approved (outside-library ref paths read-only OK; unknown-cloning→free-text = silence-is-not-a-verdict; m4a fallback needing ffmpeg+ffprobe = WATCH-ITEM, client-side wav encode is the primary path). The worker's None-not-'' ref rule and the wav-not-container recorder call were both real bugs avoided. **Debi datapoint: the random-voice-per-render happened on Qwen too — consistent: Base is ALSO a cloning model (spk_id:{} + speaker_encoder), so every unpinned render on cloning models sampled a fresh voice; CustomVoice is fixed by the serena fallback, Base+OmniVoice by the clip picker.** **GATE 2 RESULT (Debi's Mac): whisper-base one-shot = 0.90s run1 / 0.75s run2 — cold start does NOT dominate; auto-dictation v1 rides the EXISTING one-shot /api/voice/stt, NO persistent STT worker needed.** Remaining gate: the ⌘K AudioWorklet spike verdict. **QUEUED (Fable research): licensed downloadable starter voice-clip set** for Debi's 13-voice English lineup (3 US-m, 3 UK-m, 2 US-f, 2 UK-f, 3 other; LibriVox/public-domain candidates); interim = export favourites from VoiceStudio's gallery into data/voices/.

> **🎙️ REFERENCE-AUDIO VOICE CONTROL + VOICE LIBRARY SHIPPED (2026-08-13, Opus-5 builder to the Fable spec; Mac-verify PENDING, ⚠️ PENDING FABLE QA):** OmniVoice sounds far better than Qwen3-TTS but rendered a **different random voice every ▶ speak** — because a zero-shot model takes voice identity ONLY from `ref_audio` (source-verified: `omnivoice.py::Model.generate` has ref_audio/ref_text/ref_audio_max_duration_s and **no speaker table at all**). Determinism therefore = pinning a CLIP, and that is now a first-class pin sitting beside the named-voice pin. **Engine:** registry audio entries gain optional `ref_audio` (absolute path) + `ref_text` (transcript); `tts_argv` emits `--ref_audio`/`--ref_text` for tts-mlx only when pinned (both flags verified present in mlx-audio 0.4.7's `generate.py` parse_args) and **NEVER on the gguf branch** — llama-tts takes `--tts-speaker-file`, a different mechanism, so the entry-ref endpoint 400s for tts-gguf and says so. `--ref_text` is optional and worth setting: without it the engine transcribes the clip with whisper on EVERY render (generate.py:274-292 — a second model load each time). **The worker protocol gains optional `ref_audio`/`ref_text` on the `tts` command and they are PER REQUEST, exactly like `voice` — a clip change costs NO respawn** (pinned by a new fake-worker assertion: same pid across a ref change, and the fake echoes what it received). CLI parity holds: an absent reference is normalised to **None, never `''`** — `generate_audio` branches on falsy and an empty path would raise "Reference audio file not found: ". **Voice library** = `data/voices/` (a folder, not a database — gitignored via `data/`): `GET /api/voice/library`, `POST /api/voice/library/save?name=&fmt=` (RAW body like /api/voice/stt; name sanitized to an allowlisted basename ≤48 chars with the suffix FORCED from ?fmt=, 15MB cap, **never clobbers** — ' (n)' like the artifact save, because a recording cannot be re-made), `POST /api/voice/library/delete {name}` (realpath containment on data/voices, the `_deletable_target` discipline — and it **UN-PINS every entry that used the clip**, since a pin at a deleted file would only fail later, at speak time). New `POST /api/voice/entry-ref {id, path|name, ref_text?}` writes through the existing atomic `_registry_update` ('' clears; clearing the clip clears its transcript too). **Classification:** new PURE `is_cloning_model(cfg)` — `tts_model_type`/`model_type` ∈ base | voice_design | **omnivoice** (mlx-audio ROUTES on model_type via `MODEL_REMAPPING["omnivoice"]`, so a checkpoint that loads as OmniVoice necessarily declares it — this is not a name guess); `voices_for_entry` computes `cloning = is_cloning_model(cfg) OR _config_says_no_names(...)` **from the same cached config read** and `audio_entry_view` carries `cloning`, `ref_audio` (**basename only, never the path**) and `ref_text`, so the panel branches with no new probe. **Panel:** `voiceChoiceMode` gains a **'clips'** mode (declared names still WIN over the cloning verdict) — chips are the library's clip stems, gold for the pinned one, plus a `model default` chip labelled honestly ("random voice per render — pin a clip for a consistent voice"), a **🎙 record 10s** chip reusing the dictation MediaRecorder machinery (auto-stop, then an inline name box → save → **auto-pin**), a per-clip two-step ✕ delete, an optional transcript box, and a note naming the real library dir. The old "No known voice list — type the name its engine expects" dead-end is gone for these models: for a cloning checkpoint that sentence was simply WRONG. **REAL FINDING THAT SHAPED THE BUILD: the recorder encodes a WAV client-side** (`wavFromBuffer`: AudioBuffer → 16-bit mono PCM) — mlx-audio reads wav/mp3/flac with miniaudio, but **M4A/WebM go through `shutil.which("ffmpeg")` + ffprobe** (`audio_io.py`), and the bridge/worker run on a Finder-minimal PATH while our provisioned ffmpeg ships **no ffprobe** — a clip stored in the recorder's own container would have played fine in the panel and failed at render time. Tests: new **`test_voice_ref.py`** (110 checks: cloning decision table, real temp model dirs for OmniVoice/CustomVoice/Base/Kokoro/junk, argv table incl. the gguf negative and ref_text-without-clip, worker kwarg parity, suffix/size/name/containment tables, never-clobber, wiring greps), test_voice_worker.py +4 (protocol passthrough + no-respawn), test_audio_rows.js +8. **All 21 py + 11 js suites green, contract 57, CSS 499/499, ast OK, both panel JS blocks node --check clean.** ⚠️ **PENDING FABLE QA:** (1) `is_cloning_model` checks BOTH `tts_model_type` and `model_type` against one set — a checkpoint whose plain model_type is literally "base" would be called cloning (harmless direction: declared names win anyway); (2) `entry-ref` accepts an arbitrary `path` outside data/voices (Debi's own machine; read-only, never deleted); (3) a cloning-capable model we do NOT recognise (no model_type we know, no talker_config) still lands in 'text' mode rather than 'clips'; (4) the per-clip ✕ delete lives INSIDE the chip (stopPropagation + a 3s armed state) rather than as a separate surface; (5) the wav-encode fallback stores the raw m4a when `decodeAudioData` fails — it plays, but needs ffmpeg+ffprobe at render time; (6) `ref_text` capped at 500 chars; (7) a saved clip is auto-pinned onto the model it was recorded for. **UNVERIFIED until the Mac:** the installed OmniVoice config.json's exact `model_type` string (sandbox has the mlx-audio source but not Debi's checkpoint), real clone quality, and whether WKWebView's `decodeAudioData` handles its own MediaRecorder output. **Debi verify:** Models → Audio → OmniVoice → Voice row now shows a CLIP picker → 🎙 record 10s (say two sentences) → name it → it auto-pins gold → ▶ speak twice on the same reply = **the SAME voice both times**, and the second render is near-instant (the resident worker never reloaded). Then pin `model default` again → randomness returns. Ship: `./scripts/ship.sh`.

> **⚡ PERSISTENT TTS VOICE WORKER SHIPPED (2026-08-13, Opus-5 builder to the Fable spec; Mac-verify PENDING, ⚠️ PENDING FABLE QA):** every ▶ speak used to spawn `python -m mlx_audio.tts.generate` and reload the 3.6GB checkpoint, so a RE-LISTEN cost as much as the first listen (Debi-verified). New **`bridge/voice_worker.py`** — a standalone script run BY the mlx venv python, **JSON-lines over stdin/stdout** (no port, no auth surface): `ping` / `load {model}` / `tts {text,voice,out}` → `{id,ok,…}`. It calls mlx-audio's OWN python API (`mlx_audio.tts.utils.load_model` once, then `generate_audio(model=<loaded>, …)` per render — read from the 0.4.7 venv source, never shelling out to the CLI). **CLI PARITY IS PINNED**: `generate_audio`'s signature defaults DIFFER from the CLI's (`max_tokens` 1200 vs None — would truncate long replies; `voice` "af_heart" vs None — would impose a Kokoro voice on Qwen3-TTS), so `CLI_PARITY_KWARGS` reproduces argparse's dict exactly incl. the **kwargs knobs (top_p/top_k/repetition_penalty/exaggeration/gender/pitch) — a worker that quietly sounded different would be worse than a slow one. Totality: malformed line / unknown cmd / failed render all return `ok:false` and the loop keeps reading; only EOF ends it (so the worker dies with the bridge automatically — closed pipe = clean exit, no orphan). **The exit-0 invariant is carried over verbatim** (a non-empty wav is the ONLY success signal) and asserted at BOTH ends of the pipe. **stdout is protocol-only**: `main()` dup2's fd 1 onto fd 2 (catching C-level writes too) and keeps a private dup for responses, so the engine's banners/tracebacks land in `data/logs/voice-worker.log` (new, viewable in-panel) instead of corrupting the stream. **`voice.VoiceWorker`** owns the lifecycle: spawn lazily on the first tts-mlx render, respawn on MODEL change, **voice is per-request so a voice change costs no reload**, kill on default-cleared/changed + Unload + model-delete + any protocol failure, NO idle timeout; reader thread + queue gives readline a real timeout (120s render / 300s load); one in-flight request per worker (VoiceBusy→409, never a queue). **The global render lock is NARROWED** to the paths that still load per call (tts-gguf llama-tts + STT mlx_whisper) — **dictation no longer blocks on a speak render and vice versa** (closes a recorded Fable watch-item). **LEDGER HONESTY (closes the Fable-gated "the ledger ignores voice models" item, and only now became true-able):** a resident worker's `size_bytes` counts against `memory.budget_gb` via `_resident_voice_bytes()` in `_loaded_models_bytes` (new `exclude_slot="voice"`), so `/api/models`' ledger and every runner/aux gate see it; a `spawn_guard` hook (owned by app.py — voice.py must not learn to read harness.yaml) REFUSES the spawn with a 409 + "eject a chat model first" rather than blowing the budget. Surfacing is minimal: `GET /api/voice/config` gains `resident:{model,…}`, the Audio DETAIL pane shows a gold `resident` pill (reuses `.mpill.live`, zero new CSS) + an **Unload** action → new `POST /api/voice/unload` (frees RAM, does NOT clear the default — the next speak reloads). tts-gguf and STT paths UNCHANGED. Tests: new **test_voice_worker.py** (protocol purity + CLI parity + the full state machine against a FAKE worker script over REAL subprocesses: spawn/reuse/voice-change/model-change/timeout/death/load-failure/no-wav/budget/busy/lock-freedom/routing); two existing invariants HONESTLY NARROWED (`asyncio.to_thread` now wraps a functools.partial; "stt and tts share one lock" → "stt shares the ONE-SHOT lock, the worker path takes none"). All 19 py + 10 js suites green, contract 57, CSS 499/499. **⚠️ PENDING FABLE QA:** killing the worker on a tts default SWITCH (spec named only the clear case — tidier, but it is a lifecycle call); 300s load timeout vs the spec's flat 120s; `VoiceBudget` as a new VoiceError subclass mapped to 409; the panel re-rendering the Audio detail pane only when the resident id CHANGES (an unconditional re-render on the periodic refresh would eat the free-text voice input mid-typing); reusing `.mpill.live` gold for a THIRD meaning (live / default / resident); `voice-worker` added to `_LOG_NAMES` + panel LOG_SOURCES. **UNVERIFIED until the Mac:** the mlx-audio python API path itself (mlx is Apple-only — the worker cannot run in-sandbox), real second-render latency, and whether any model family prints to fd 1 in a way the dup2 misses. Ship: `./scripts/ship.sh`.

> **🎤 VOICE PHASE D SHIPPED — DICTATION (2026-08-08, Opus-5 builder to the Fable spec; Mac-verify PENDING, ⚠️ PENDING FABLE QA):** the composer gains a **`● talk`** button (between the textarea and Send, both resized to one shared smaller rule; hidden until an STT default exists) → mic → MediaRecorder → `POST /api/voice/stt` → transcript **APPENDED into the message box, NEVER auto-sent** (a misheard word must stay editable). Recording state = `■ M:SS` (1s tick, `--bad` glyph only, hard auto-stop at 60s); `…` while transcribing; errors reuse the ▶ speak `speakFlash` idiom. **bridge/voice.py** gains the STT half in tts_render's exact shape — pure `stt_argv` (console-script form `mlx_whisper <audio> --model <path> -f json -o <dir> --verbose False` per spec §6: the CLI WRITES FILES, it does not print the transcript; ⚠️ plus a library-import FALLBACK `python -c "…mlx_whisper.transcribe(…, path_or_hf_repo=…)"` for a venv whose console script is missing — the one thing we cannot verify in-sandbox), `stt_transcribe` (temp dir under data/tmp always cleaned, **the SAME global render lock** as TTS, 120s timeout, non-wav input converted via ffmpeg to 16k mono), and the exit-0 invariant carried over verbatim: mlx_whisper prints a traceback and exits 0, so **an empty transcript IS the failure signal** (`stt_read_output`: json file first, stdout second, `''` = error). ffmpeg is resolved but **never installed at request time** (ensure_ffmpeg.sh is an install-time helper) — no ffmpeg + non-wav ⇒ a short VoiceError naming voicebox/voicestudio; a **wav needs no external tool at all**, so curl works on a bare machine. New `POST /api/voice/stt` takes the recording as the RAW body with `?fmt=` (400 no-default/bad-format, 413 >25MB, 409 lock busy, 500 + engine log tail). `build.mlx_whisper_pin: "0.4.3"` + install_mlx.sh installs it into the EXISTING data/mlx-venv (shares mlx/numpy, pulls no torch) and WARNs loudly if the console script is absent. **Shell:** `requestMediaCapturePermissionFor` WKUIDelegate grant added beside `runOpenPanelWith` (mic granted, camera denied — same class of silent no-op that broke ⊕ attach), and build_app.sh's Info.plist heredoc gains `NSMicrophoneUsageDescription`. ⚠️ **ship.sh does NOT regenerate Info.plist** → a one-time `plutil -insert` + re-codesign on the installed app (exact commands in the session report); ship.sh DOES recompile the shell (main.swift changed). Tests: new **test_voice_stt.py** (both argv forms, the full suffix/conversion decision table, caps, read-output precedence, lock sharing, bridge wiring read from app.py source), new **test_talk_helpers.js** (talkTime/talkMime + panel wiring facts), new contract **test_mlx_whisper_contract.py** (skips cleanly in-sandbox; on the Mac runs `--help` and pins --model/-f/-o/--verbose + json, so a pin-bump trips). **⚠️ PENDING FABLE QA:** the library fallback existing at all; ONE shared lock for tts+stt (dictation now blocks on a speak render and vice versa); the `● talk` size rounding (12.5px/7px-15px −30% → 9px/5px-10px — 8.75px would be a fractional size); mp4-before-webm MediaRecorder preference; `ffmpeg_bin` keeping a `shutil.which` first probe (now backed by an EXPLICIT candidate list per the Finder-PATH rule — this narrowed an existing test_voice_tts.py invariant from "the module contains no which()" to "the ENGINE resolvers contain none"); mlx-whisper NOT added to the fat wheelhouse (same asymmetry mlx-audio already has); 60s recording cap; camera denied outright. **UNVERIFIED until a whisper model is downloaded + the app rebuilt:** whether WKWebView's MediaRecorder actually produces mp4 here, real transcription latency/quality, and whether the mmproj-free whisper MLX repos satisfy whole-repo download mode.

> **✅ FABLE QA OF THE 2026-08-08 OPUS-5 STRETCH (Phase D + denylist fix + contract skip) — COMPLETE:** ALL VERDICTS APPROVED. (1) `_mlx_repo_files` allowlist→**denylist**: correct, and the LESSON generalizes (fail toward a few KB too many, never a dead model); one watch-item recorded — `_MLX_SKIP_EXT` drops `.wav`/`.mp3`, so a model repo whose REQUIRED assets are audio files would break (none known; voice files ship as .pt/.safetensors); revisit only if hit. (2) contract `_help()` "usage" gate: right call (cross-machine venv shebang ⇒ skip, not 4 failures). (3) Phase D tags: library-import fallback KEEP (guarded, cheap insurance); **one shared TTS+STT lock KEEP for v1** (one GPU, prevents concurrent multi-GB loads; revisit only if dictation-blocks-speak annoys in practice); 9px/5px-10px rounding fine; mp4-before-webm correct for WKWebView; ffmpeg_bin which-probe + explicit candidate list satisfies the Finder-PATH rule and the test narrowing was honest; mlx-whisper NOT in fat wheelhouse = consistent with the mlx-audio asymmetry (both become a single fat-rebuild line item, recorded); 60s cap + camera-deny correct. (4) **TORCH MYSTERY SOLVED (venv dist-info METADATA read): `mlx-whisper` DECLARES `torch` as a hard dependency** — not resolver creep, not Kokoro/misaki. VERDICT: ACCEPT + document — the torch-free rule targeted optional bloat; this is a declared upstream requirement of our chosen STT engine, isolated to data/mlx-venv, never in the fat wheelhouse (~2GB disk cost). Torch-free alternative if ever wanted = whisper.cpp (new binary + pin — a full slice, not queued). (5) CustomVoice diagnosis (voice-cloning model, needs ref audio, will never work via one-shot text) — correct, belongs to a future clone feature or VoiceStudio. **NEXT (Fable design, spec'd below): the VOICE PICKER** — Qwen3-TTS-mlx picks a random named voice when `--voice` is absent (Debi observed it).

> **🔬 AUDIO RESEARCH REPORT ACCEPTED + BUILD ORDER APPROVED BY DEBI (2026-08-08, Opus-5 research, citations in the session log):** **RETRACTION — Fable's CustomVoice diagnosis was WRONG, backwards in fact:** `tts_model_type` in config.json is the discriminator — **CustomVoice = the NAMED-VOICES model** (spk_id: serena/vivian/ryan/aiden/dylan(Beijing)/eric(Sichuan)/uncle_fu/sohee/ono_anna; NO speaker encoder; unmatched name raises LOUDLY listing valid voices), **Base = the CLONING variant** (spk_id:{}, speaker_encoder present, unmatched name silently ignored — Debi's A/B, now source-confirmed). **Chelsie/Ethan exist in NO checkpoint — README fiction**; KNOWN_VOICES corrected to the real 9 (+ tests). mlx-audio 0.4.7 already exposes `--instruct` (CustomVoice emotion / VoiceDesign). **IMMEDIATE Debi win: set the already-installed CustomVoice as default TTS and pin a real voice.** OTHER KEY FINDINGS: full mlx-audio TTS family table w/ authoritative licenses — 🚫 Spark-TTS = CC-BY-NC-SA, 🚫 Voxtral-TTS = CC-BY-NC (20 named voices, tempting, disallowed), OuteTTS is a PER-REPO license trap (1.0=apache, Llama-OuteTTS=NC), no-license-tag repos (kitten/soprano-1.1/ggml-org GGUF) get badged not offered; llama-tts@b10295 = Qwen3-TTS ONLY, gguf lane structurally has no named voices (our 400 + note correct as built); Nemotron/NeMo TTS = NO (torch framework venv; mlx-audio's NVIDIA entries are all STT — parakeet/nemotron-streaming ARE interesting for streaming STT later). AUTO-DICTATION architecture settled: **browser-side silero-VAD (MIT, 2.2MB ONNX, self-hosted vad-web + ort-wasm single-threaded) → per-utterance client-encoded 16kHz mono wav → EXISTING /api/voice/stt** (kills the MediaRecorder chunk-container hazard — W3C-confirmed chunks aren't independently decodable — AND the whisper silence-hallucination class AND the ffmpeg dependency); LocalAgreement/streaming REJECTED (needs persistent model, collides w/ the global render lock); ⚠️ TWO GATES BEFORE BUILD: AudioWorklet-in-WKWebView spike (~20 lines, the make-or-break unknown — same silent-no-op class as ⊕/mic) + cold-start measurement (`time` same wav twice — if subprocess cold-start dominates, the REAL slice is a persistent STT worker and priorities reorder). HF SEARCH: `?library=` is SILENTLY IGNORED (never send); use `?filter=mlx-audio|mlx + pipeline_tag=text-to-speech` UNION of two queries (mlx-audio alone MISSES Kokoro); **tags lie — classify on config.json**: mlx-whisper shape = the 11-key ModelDimensions set (require n_mels/n_audio_state/n_audio_head/n_audio_layer/n_vocab/n_text_state), transformers veto keys = _name_or_path/architectures/transformers_version/num_mel_bins/d_model/encoder_layers/decoder_layers/is_encoder_decoder (whisper-medium trips 8/8; a whole-repo transformers download would also pull ~12GB of 4 redundant weight copies — unfixable at filename layer, the config probe is THE fix); Kokoro's 54 voices live in `voices/*.pt` files NOT config (two discovery mechanisms, one UI); our downloader ALREADY uses recursive=true (researcher's dir-skip concern not live) and the npz fix shipped today. **APPROVED BUILD ORDER: T1** downloader npz✓(done) + config-probe classifier + CustomVoice-as-qwen3-default/KNOWN_VOICES✓(done); **T2** config-derived voices (note must NAME the CustomVoice sibling when spk_id empty; surface spk_is_dialect) + Audio HF search w/ license badging; **T3** AudioWorklet spike → cold-start measurement → auto-dictation v1. Auto mode is ALSO the trigger to revisit the shared TTS+STT lock.

Fix applied live: run `./scripts/install_mlx.sh` + `bash scripts/ensure_ffmpeg.sh data/mlx-venv` from the SNAPSHOT dir. TO CLOSE PROPERLY (queued): panel-triggered "update MLX runtime" action, or ship.sh warning when repo/snapshot venv versions diverge. (2) **RESCAN CLASSIFIER FALSE-POSITIVE**: `openai/whisper-medium` in the HF cache is a TRANSFORMERS checkpoint, classified stt-mlx by the audio scan (whisper token + config.json + safetensors) — mlx-whisper dies with `ModelDimensions.__init__() unexpected keyword '_name_or_path'`. QUEUED FIX (with the config-derived-voices slice): hf-cache whisper entries must verify MLX shape — require "mlx" in the repo name OR probe config.json for ModelDimensions keys (n_mels/n_audio_state) vs transformers keys (_name_or_path/architectures). IMMEDIATE Debi fix: Get **whisper-base-mlx** (137MB, real MLX conversion) from the starters → set default STT. ALSO RECORDED: **automatic dictation mode** (VAD/hands-free/streaming, à la the awesome-whisper ecosystem apps — all open source, silero-VAD MIT) = Fable-design enhancement; manual click-stop stays as the base mode. ffmpeg confirmed on PATH (/opt/homebrew — user's own, not shadowed).

> **🔎 VOICE-PICKER VERDICT (2026-08-08, Debi's A/B + config read settled it):** the picker CHAIN is fully correct (registry pin → argv `--voice Ethan` → engine echo), BUT `mlx-community/Qwen3-TTS-12Hz-1.7B-Base-8bit` declares **`talker_config.spk_id = {}` — NO named voices exist in this checkpoint**; engine source (`qwen3_tts.py:385`) shows an unrecognized name SILENTLY falls through to the unconditioned default (and still echoes the requested name — the echo proves nothing). Chelsie/Ethan came from the model card and do NOT apply to this Base conversion; both A/B renders = same female default. "Base" = voice via **ref_audio cloning**, not names. **NEXT SLICE (Fable design, queued): config-derived voices** — `voices_for()` must read the model's own config.json `spk_id` (+ `spk_is_dialect`) when present and show chips ONLY for declared voices (static table = fallback for undeclared models); a model with empty spk_id shows an honest "this model has no named voices — voice control needs reference audio" note; OPTIONAL follow-up = ref-audio voice support (pick a wav → engine `--ref_audio`, also unlocks CustomVoice + llama.cpp `--tts-speaker-file`) — needs a Fable UX spec. KNOWN_VOICES table demoted to offer-of-last-resort.

> **🗣️ VOICE PICKER SHIPPED (2026-08-08, Opus-5 built to Fable design, Fable QA APPROVED all 8 tags; Mac-verify pending):** the voice is a property of the MODEL choice ⇒ stored as `voice` on the registry audio entry (tts_argv already consumed it — verified+pinned both directions: passed only for tts-mlx, never tts-gguf). `KNOWN_VOICES` = **an OFFER, not a whitelist** (qwen3-tts → Chelsie/Ethan from the model card; kokoro → 8 ids, recon-level from hexgrad VOICES.md; unlisted names still validate — a stale table must never block a real voice; a typo degrades to engine fallback). New `POST /api/voice/entry-voice` (validates ≤64 chars, empty clears = key REMOVED not null — the yaml-null lesson applied to json; 400 on tts-gguf: "llama.cpp models have no voice parameter"); new atomic `_registry_update`. Panel: Voice row in the Audio DETAIL pane only (chips w/ gold `.mp-act.on` for known families, free-text+set for unknown mlx, faint fixed-by-model note for gguf; "model default" chip = cleared, labelled honestly "mlx-audio picks a random named voice per render"); composer popover shows a display-only `voice: <name>` pill. **BEST CATCH (beyond brief, Fable-approved): `seed_registry.merge()` would have silently UN-PINNED every local/hf-cache voice on one RESCAN click** (scans read files; the voice is a user decision living nowhere on disk) → `_keep_voice` carry-forward mirroring the ctx rule; download-source entries were never at risk. `setEntryVoice` stops a playing clip (old-voice audio must not outlive the switch). Tests: voice_tts +38, audio_rows.js +13, audio_registry +5 (rescan preservation); 57 contract, all suites green, CSS 491/491. Verify: Audio tab → Qwen3-TTS-MLX → Voice row → pin **Ethan** → ▶ speak twice = same voice; model default = randomness returns; RESCAN keeps the pin.

> **🐛 TTS "produced no audio" ROOT-CAUSED + FIXED (2026-08-08, Debi's engine traceback did it):** every MLX TTS render failed `render_ok`. The exit-0 invariant did its job (honest error, no hang) but the cause was OURS, in the DOWNLOAD not the engine: the direct-engine run printed `Warning: Could not load tokenizer: 'vocab' and 'merges' must be both be from memory or both filenames` → `ValueError: Tokenizer not loaded. Call post_load_hook first.` **`_mlx_repo_files` was an ALLOWLIST** (`*.safetensors` OR basename startswith `tokenizer` OR `*.json`). `mlx-community/Qwen3-TTS-12Hz-1.7B-Base-8bit` ships NO `tokenizer.json` — its tokenizer is **`vocab.json` + `merges.txt`** (HF tree API verified). vocab.json matched `*.json` and downloaded; **merges.txt matched nothing and was silently skipped** ⇒ a model dir that looks complete, loads weights + speech_tokenizer fine, then dies at generate time. FIX: `_mlx_repo_files` is now a **DENYLIST** (`_MLX_SKIP_EXACT` .gitattributes/.gitignore/.DS_Store/license/notice; `_MLX_SKIP_EXT` .md/.png/.jpg/.jpeg/.gif/.svg/.webp/.mp3/.wav/.pdf/.gguf) + skips non-file tree entries — whole-repo mode now really means the whole repo. **LESSON: an allowlist over third-party model repos re-breaks on every new model family; fail toward a few KB too many, never toward a dead model.** Verified against the REAL Qwen3-TTS listing (merges.txt/vocab.json/speech_tokenizer/* in; README/.gitattributes out). ⚠️ **Existing broken downloads do NOT self-heal** — re-download the model dir (or drop the missing file in by hand). Also fixed: `test_mlx_whisper_contract._help()` treats a launcher whose shebang points at another machine's interpreter (`exec: …python3: not found` — a Mac-built venv read from the sandbox) as a SKIP rather than 4 failures; real `--help` always contains "usage".

> **Full system reference: `docs/HARNESS-INTERNALS.md`** — how the harness actually works (ports, builds/snapshot rule, harness.yaml keys, the four chat lanes, session stores, models/registry, path-guard, endpoints, ops gotchas, install footprint, test index). Code-derived; read it before changing anything.

> **🚢 STANDING OPS RULE (2026-08-07, ends the stale-snapshot fights): ALL code ships via `./scripts/ship.sh`** — copies panel+bridge+scripts+guards+policies into the fat snapshot (never harness.yaml/data), recompiles the Swift shell ONLY if app/main.swift is newer than the installed binary, then quits the app AND **kills the :8700 bridge LISTENER** (the trap: the bridge is a separate process that outlives the app — quit app ≠ restart bridge; a pre-existing bridge gets ADOPTED on relaunch and serves stale code forever), relaunches, waits, prints a sanity fingerprint. Never hand-write cp/pkill ship sequences again. **NO EXCEPTIONS (Debi directive): every change ships this way — a one-line panel tweak included. There is no "too small"; "quick cp + ⌘R" instructions are FORBIDDEN. The verify instruction to Debi is always exactly `./scripts/ship.sh`.** **MANIFEST MERGE (added 2026-08-07 after the voice components exposed the gap): ship.sh still never copies harness.yaml wholesale (the snapshot's copy holds LIVE state — installed flags, runner.model, aux.model), but it now runs an ADDITIVE python/yaml merge — any top-level key, `components.*` or `build.*` present in the repo but MISSING from the snapshot is added (new components forced `installed:false`/`enabled:false`, since install state is per-machine); existing keys/values are NEVER touched, the snapshot is backed up first, and the merge no-ops silently when nothing is missing. Without this a newly-added component's card simply never appeared in Mission Control — a silent failure that would have hit EVERY future component. ⚠️ **PENDING FABLE QA — GOVERNANCE NOTE (Debi flagged it, correctly, 2026-08-07): `ship.sh` is the critical ops path, so changing how the manifest reaches the snapshot is a Fable-lane decision, not a builder's.** The Opus-5 builder session that added it even had its own agent flag "Fable's call whether ship.sh should merge new `build.*` keys" and overrode that flag because the missing cards were blocking Debi's verification. Fable to review: (a) additive-merge-only semantics (never touch existing keys/values); (b) forcing `installed:false`/`enabled:false` on newly-merged components; (c) the yaml round-trip losing the SNAPSHOT copy's comments/ordering on the one write that changes something (repo copy, the source of truth, keeps its comments — same accepted property as the `~/.hermes/config.yaml` write); (d) the timestamped `.bak-<ts>` snapshots accumulating in the snapshot dir with no pruning; (e) the python-resolution order (bridge-venv python → system python3) and the LOUD warn when no pyyaml exists — an earlier revision skipped SILENTLY, which hid this exact bug for one cycle.** **ALSO (Debi directive, corrected twice): every file deliverable — docs, dmg, specs, anything built — gets SURFACED to Debi as a file card the moment it exists (cowork present_files), never left to be asked for.**

> **✅ FABLE 5 QA OF THE VOICE BATCH — COMPLETE (2026-08-07):** identity note first: the whole voice session ran on Fable-5 runtime under an "Opus 5 builder" framing — work was delegated to sub-agents per doctrine and properly tagged, but the session-start declaration was wrong; recorded, not repeating. **VERDICTS — approved as built:** searxng-precedent shallow-clone for optional components (correct: bootstrap must never pull multi-GB optionals; the `.git` precheck skip is name-scoped); uv-vs-pip split (each mirrors its own upstream's install contract — uniformity here would be wrong); ship.sh ADDITIVE manifest merge semantics incl. forcing installed:false/enabled:false on new components + LOUD pyyaml warn (the silent-skip revision was the bug; governance breach acknowledged — merge was Fable-lane, built by builder under verification pressure — but the artifact is correct); voice `/api/voice/*` as new endpoints (browse is one hardcoded server; a keyed table deserves its own surface); 409-on-enable + stale-url delete+re-add (never leave a silently-wrong registration); Hermes write-skip-when-unchanged (strictly better: fewer comment-losing writes); `ok`+`partial` semantics (honest); TRANSLATE_* not stealing the UI's provider choice; OMNIVOICE_API_URL export (real bug found by builder — approved); ffmpeg extended to voicestudio (whisperx/demucs shell out by name — right call); pin-read-after-PATH-branches in ensure_* (user's own tool must never be blocked by a missing key); copy-not-symlink ffmpeg; explicit-path uv resolution + THE GENERAL RULE (Finder-minimal PATH ⇒ every bridge-spawned script resolves external tools by explicit path list — now standing); venv fallback chains + `python -m pip` everywhere; setuptools<80 post-hoc best-effort (acceptable — uv sync re-pruning is theoretical until a reinstall, and the line self-heals); ports hardcoded in _NOTES/plan strings while start scripts read yaml (accepted: contract tests pin the ports so drift trips loudly); 5-tab strip + always-visible optional tabs (KEEP — hiding segments would add index-conditional complexity to every branch for cosmetic gain; revisit only if Debi finds it noisy); vsWV/vbWV naming; no staleness-reload for voice tabs (correct — that mechanism is Hermes-WS-specific). **TWO FIXES BY FABLE:** (1) `voicebox-install` promoted into `_LOG_NAMES` + panel LOG_SOURCES (label "voicebox install"; also renamed voicestudio's log label 'voice'→'voicestudio' for symmetry) — an install log for the known-fragile component MUST be viewable in-panel; (2) ship.sh manifest-merge backups now pruned to the 5 newest (unbounded .bak accumulation). Validated: ast OK, ship.sh bash -n OK, CSS balanced, both JS blocks node --check OK, contract 42/42, voice-mcp 36/36, port-kill ALL PASS (proxy env cleared). **STILL OPEN (unchanged): RAM ledger ignores voice models (Fable-gated, needs a spec); 2nd-Mac dmg test; PAT revoke.** **VOICE-CAPABILITY DESIGN QUESTION (Debi raised it; Fable verdict): the MCP toggles STAY OFF pending a decision, and the question is correctly framed as "voice app vs voice capability."** Direction I endorse: agents-speaking/listening should eventually be a HARNESS-NATIVE capability on MIT parts we largely already ship (mlx-audio is ALREADY in the fat wheelhouse as an mlx-vlm dep; llama.cpp ships llama-tts/OuteTTS at our pinned build; whisper via mlx-whisper/whisper.cpp; Kokoro Apache-2.0 is the quality-per-MB standout; AVOID Coqui XTTS — non-commercial license, dead org). VoiceStudio then remains what it is today: an optional AGPL creative studio in a tab, never wired into agents. Needs a Fable spec (runner-adjacent TTS/STT slot vs aux-style process; ledger accounting; composer UX is Fable-lane anyway). NOT started — recorded as the roadmap successor to the voice item.

switch/aux 400 on an audio id; ledger ignores audio. harness.yaml gains `voice:{tts_model,stt_model}`; `build.mlx_audio_pin: 0.4.7` + install_mlx.sh pins it (provisioned venv has 0.4.4 ⇒ upgrade on next install_mlx run). **BLOCKER FIXED BY THE BUILDER: ship.sh copied only `bridge/app.py`**, so voice.py would never have reached the snapshot and the bridge would fail to import — now loops `bridge/*.py` (⚠️ PENDING FABLE QA: ops-path edit, additive/same-destination). **Phase A (Models → Audio tab):** `CHAT MODELS | AUDIO` sub-tab in the caps-tab grammar (persisted `localStorage['harness-models-section']`, chat pane byte-identical to before), audio rows w/ tts/stt + gold `default` + engine + size pills, detail w/ Set-default-TTS/STT + Clear + guarded Delete, and a **Get voice models** section with the five recon-verified starters (Qwen3-TTS GGUF Q4_K_M **+ mmproj Q8_0**, Qwen3-TTS-MLX-8bit, Kokoro-82M-bf16 *with the misaki/torch warning on the card*, whisper-base-mlx, whisper-large-v3-turbo) through the EXISTING download manager. **TWO MORE REAL BUGS THE BUILDER CAUGHT:** (1) the lone-mmproj-sibling heuristic only fires when a repo has exactly ONE mmproj, but the Qwen3-TTS GGUF repo ships several quants ⇒ `/api/dl/start` gained an explicit `mmproj` override, without which llama-tts would get no projector; (2) deleting a model now also CLEARS it as a voice default, else harness.yaml points at deleted weights and only fails at speak time. `seed_registry.py` gains a deliberately NARROW audio scan (MLX shape needs config.json+safetensors AND a family token; GGUF needs a non-mmproj gguf AND an mmproj sibling AND a TTS token — a false positive would make a chat model VANISH from the chat list) + new read-only `audio-hf-cache` source. **Phase C (composer):** `AUDIO off ⌄` chip beside the model chip → `#audio-pop` reusing the `#model-pop` CSS byte-for-byte (row 0 Off, **tts-gguf/llama.cpp rows FIRST per Debi**, full untruncated ids + engine/size/gold-default pills, `.mp-err` inline errors, bottom `Audio models…` jump that sets the Models sub-tab, empty state "No voice models yet — get one"); closes on selection/Esc/outside-mousedown/OUTSIDE-scroll/resize/lane-switch. **`▶ speak` as the FIRST chip in every assistant `.msg-acts`** (all lanes incl. Hermes; rendered only when a TTS default exists) → `…` → `■ stop`, one clip app-wide via `currentAudio`, object URL revoked on every exit path, errors flash `err` w/ the server message as title, empty-after-strip flashes `nothing to speak`. Pure `speakText()` strips fenced blocks (+ bare backticks) and is unit-tested. NO autoplay in v1. **Tests: contract 50, 16 py + 8 js suites all green**, new test_voice_tts.py (107 checks), test_audio_registry.py (82), test_audio_rows.js (18), test_speak_text.js (23), new test_llama_tts_contract.py (skips cleanly in-sandbox, runs `--help` on the Mac and asserts `-mv` is ABSENT so a pin-bump trips). CSS 483/483, both JS blocks node --check clean, ast OK. **UNVERIFIED until a model is downloaded:** real render, audio quality/latency, wav playback in WKWebView, whether the two whisper MLX repos satisfy whole-repo mode (a `weights.npz`-only repo would 400). **⚠️ PENDING FABLE QA (builder tags):** ship.sh `bridge/*.py` loop; defensive voice import (a syntax error in voice.py degrades to 503 instead of failing loudly); global vs per-engine lock; `is_audio_entry` matching kind OR audio-format; new 400 on switch/aux for audio ids; `_set_yaml_scalar` appending a missing block; mlx-audio pin asymmetry (install_mlx pins 0.4.7 but the fat wheelhouse/firstrun still get it transitively — Fable's call whether to bump both + rebuild fat); `default` pill reuses `.mpill.live` gold; HF-cache scanning at all; `SEARCH/DOWNLOAD` chip hidden on the Audio tab; AUDIO chip deliberately NOT gold when on; speakText stripping bare backticks + dropping text after an unterminated fence. **🚨 INCIDENT + FIX — ship.sh's yaml round-trip broke EVERY model load (2026-08-07, caused by Fable's manifest merge, found by Debi, fixed same session):** symptom = `FAILED to load <model> — reverted to . ERROR: runner.binary is not executable: null` in both the Models detail and the chat lane; `/api/models/switch` returned 200 (the bridge was fine) and the failure was downstream in start_component.sh. ROOT CAUSE: `yaml.safe_dump` writes a Python `None` as the literal text `null`, so the ONE merge write that added the voice components rewrote the snapshot's `runner.binary:` (empty = auto-discover) as `runner.binary: null`, and the shell awk/sed readers took the 4-char string "null" as a real path. Every intentionally-empty key was hit (runner.binary, aux.model, the new voice.* slots). **This is the exact hazard Fable's own QA flagged as "(c) the yaml round-trip" — the note said "loses comments" and MISSED that it also converts empty→null; comments are cosmetic, this broke the product.** FIX: ship.sh registers a None representer (`d.represent_scalar('tag:yaml.org,2002:null','')`) before dumping, so an empty key round-trips as an empty key — sandbox-verified before/after. REPAIR for an already-poisoned snapshot: `sed -i '' 's/: null$/:/' "$DST/harness.yaml"` (verified to re-parse correctly; a `.prenullfix` copy was kept). **STANDING LESSON: any machine round-trip of harness.yaml must preserve EMPTY-as-EMPTY, not just comments — the shell readers are text-level and treat `null` as data.** Debi confirmed chat models load again (90.8 tok/s).

> **🔊 VOICE PHASE A SHIPPED — Models → AUDIO tab (2026-08-07, Opus-5 builder to the Fable spec; Mac-verify PENDING, ⚠️ PENDING FABLE QA):** the Models view gains a two-chip sub-tab strip in the EXISTING `caps-tabs`/`caps-tab` grammar — **Chat models | Audio** — show/hide only over containers rendered from the SAME `/api/models` payload (`installed` vs the Phase-B `audio` array), persisted in `localStorage['harness-models-section']`, default `chat`; the chat panes are never rebuilt so today's UI is unchanged. **AUDIO tab** reuses `.mrow`/`.mpill`/`.md-*` with **zero new CSS primitives** (three existing selectors extended to `#models-audio-main`/`#models-audio-left`/`#models-audio-detail`; CSS 488/488 unchanged): rows = full id + pills `tts`/`stt`, gold `default`, engine (`llama.cpp`/`mlx`), provenance badge, size; detail = id/engine/format/size/source/path (+ mmproj path) with **Set default TTS/STT** → `POST /api/voice/config`, **Clear default** (posts "") when it IS the default, and **Delete** through the existing guarded two-step (`_deletable_target` already covers audio: source local/download + realpath under data/models). **"Get voice models"** offers the five recon-verified starters through the EXISTING download manager (progress/pause/resume/cancel free, `#downloads-sec` is a shared sibling so it shows on both tabs and still hoists above the pane while active): Qwen3-TTS GGUF **pair**, Qwen3-TTS MLX, Kokoro (WITH the misaki/torch caveat printed on the card), whisper base + large-v3-turbo; licences on every card. **THE DOWNLOAD→REGISTRY SEAM (the design call): an explicit `voice_format` hint travels with the Get click** (`/api/dl/start` gained optional `voice_format` ∈ tts-gguf|tts-mlx|stt-mlx and `mmproj:"<file>.gguf"`) rather than sniffing filenames on the way back — a filename sniff deciding "TTS backbone or chat model?" would, on a false positive, make a CHAT model vanish from every chat list. New pure `voice.audio_download_entry(base, fmt)` converts the existing `_gguf_registry_entry`/`_mlx_registry_entry` output into ONE `kind:"audio"` entry (gguf pair ⇒ path=backbone + mmproj=projector, never two rows; vision/ctx dropped). The explicit mmproj is REQUIRED because the old lone-sibling heuristic only fires when a repo has exactly one mmproj and the Qwen3-TTS repo ships several quants — without it llama-tts would have no projector. **`scripts/seed_registry.py`** gains a deliberately NARROW audio scan so **Rescan** finds voice models already on the Mac: pure `audio_format_for(dir_name, filenames)` (MLX shape = config.json + safetensors AND a family token in the folder name → `whisper*`⇒stt-mlx, `*tts*`/`kokoro`/`outetts`⇒tts-mlx; GGUF shape requires BOTH a non-mmproj gguf and an mmproj sibling PLUS a TTS token — a lone gguf and every vision chat pair classify as None), `scan_audio_local` (data/models ⇒ source `local`, app-owned/deletable), `scan_audio_hf_cache` (~/.cache/huggingface/hub, MLX shape only ⇒ NEW rescanned source `audio-hf-cache`, read-only), `local_entries_for()` so a folder matching BOTH scanners yields exactly one (audio) entry, and `merge()` extended with `RESCANNED_SOURCES` + drop-on-collision for cache entries. Also: **deleting a model now clears it as a voice default** (`was_voice` in `/api/models/delete`) — a harness.yaml pointing at deleted weights would only fail later, at speak time. **Tests: new `bridge/tests/test_audio_registry.py` 82/82** — the builder, the classifier (every NEGATIVE case is load-bearing), temp-tree scans, merge semantics, and **the invariant asserted against the REAL `api_models()` handler with a temp ROOT: an audio entry NEVER appears in `installed`, including one that has an audio `format` but no `kind`**. Full sweep green (16 py + 6 js suites; contract **50 passed**), ast OK ×3, node --check both panel blocks, CSS 488/488. **⚠️ PENDING FABLE QA:** (1) the `default` pill reuses `.mpill.live` (gold FILLED, same as the live-model pill) — deliberate "this one is active" parity, but the class name now carries two meanings; (2) the audio family token lists (`tts`/`kokoro`/`outetts`, `whisper`) ARE the whole safety margin — adding a generic word like "voice"/"audio" would start eating chat models; (3) HF-cache scanning at all (Debi asked for "rescan what's already on the computer") — mitigated by MLX-shape-only + token gate + read-only source, but it does index files outside our tree; (4) the `SEARCH / DOWNLOAD` chip is HIDDEN on the Audio tab (HF browse is chat-quant-shaped; the Audio tab has its curated Get list instead) — a future "search HF for voice models" is a separate slice; (5) `voice_format` as a `/api/dl/start` body key rather than a separate endpoint (keeps ONE download machine); (6) a bad hint at completion logs and registers the CHAT-shaped entry rather than losing the download. **UNVERIFIED until a real download:** every starter's exact filenames/sizes, and whether the two whisper MLX repos satisfy whole-repo mode's `safetensors + config.json` precondition (a repo shipping `weights.npz` instead would 400 — recon-level confidence only). Ship: `./scripts/ship.sh`.

> **🎙️ VOICE RECON COMPLETE — Phase 0 of FABLE-VOICE-TABS-SPEC (2026-08-07, Opus-5 builder, web-evidenced, NO code written yet):** **VoiceStudio = `debpalash/VoiceStudio`, formerly "OmniVoice Studio"** — every internal id/env var/data dir is still `omnivoice` (⚠️ this is the same family as the stray `K2-FSA/OMNIVOICE` HF-cache entry that poisoned our MLX `/v1/models` probe on 2026-08-06 — Debi had installed it before). 9.7k★, pushed TODAY, **AGPL-3.0-only** (⚠️ SPEC-MANDATED FLAG TO DEBI; same posture as SearXNG — composing over HTTP without modifying triggers nothing; a paid commercial license exists for closed embedding; bundled `omnivoice/` TTS model code stays Apache-2.0 from `k2-fsa/OmniVoice`). WIRING FIT IS EXCELLENT: single FastAPI process serves **API + built SPA on one port 3900** (`backend/main.py:1290-1323` mounts `frontend/dist`), **binds 127.0.0.1 BY DEFAULT** (`main.py:1419`, explicit comment: loopback because "VoiceStudio ships no authentication"), `OMNIVOICE_BIND_HOST`/`OMNIVOICE_PORT` overrides, `GET /health`, exit code 78 = port-in-use, **MCP streamable-HTTP mounted in-process at `/mcp`** (+ stdio shim `python -m backend.mcp_shim`; tools generate_speech/clone_voice/transcribe/list_voices/list_personalities/list_languages/check_health; disable via `OMNIVOICE_MCP_DISABLE=1`), **optional LLM is OpenAI-compatible** (pyproject names Ollama/LM Studio/vLLM → we aim it at runner :6767; core TTS/ASR needs no LLM), telemetry = PostHog **opt-in, default OFF**, no API keys, offline after model download. PIN = **`v0.4.2`** (2026-07-27; ⚠️ recon file reads came from `main` — re-verify at the tag). COSTS/RISKS: python≥3.11 + **uv**; torch 2.8 + transformers 5.3 + whisperx + pyannote<4 + demucs + sherpa-onnx + mlx trio ⇒ **~5-8GB venv + ~2.4GB base models** (docs say 10GB free min); **`setuptools<80` hard cap** (whisperx needs pkg_resources) vs our wheelhouse's 82.0.1/<83 — separate venvs make it safe but the FAT wheelhouse needs a THIRD constrained setuptools; **two direct-URL wheels** (`kittentts`, `en_core_web_sm`) that a name-based `--find-links` wheelhouse CANNOT resolve ⇒ fat/offline install is where this fights us; SPA needs a **`bun` build** (new prereq; if `frontend/dist` is absent the backend still boots and serves a stub — graceful); **global ⌘⇧Space dictation + tray + floating widget live in the Tauri/Rust shell, NOT the backend** ⇒ a webview tab gets Studio/Dub/Audiobook + full HTTP/MCP but **no system-wide dictation** (honest capability loss to tell Debi). **Voicebox = `jamiepine/voicebox`** — MIT ✅, 30.1k★ (prior "45k" REFUTED), but **STALE: last push 2026-04-26, 449 open issues**; genuine headless server (`python -m backend.main --host 127.0.0.1 --port 17493`; ⚠️ argparse default is **8000** — 17493 is convention only, MUST be passed), `/health`, FastMCP at `/mcp` (voicebox.speak/transcribe/list_captures/list_profiles), MLX on arm64, **zero auth anywhere** (loopback mandatory; never expose via M5 Tailscale without a proxy). BLOCKING RISK: **5 packages need `--no-deps`/git-URL/custom-index** (chatterbox-tts, hume-tada, mlx-audio, Zipvoice/linacodec, piper-phonemize) — mutually conflicting pins that **will not resolve into our offline wheelhouse**; and its SPA needs bun build + a manual `web/dist → <root>/frontend` copy no non-Docker path performs. **BUILDER RECOMMENDATION (⚠️ PENDING FABLE QA): ship VoiceStudio ONLY; DROP/defer Voicebox** — the two overlap almost entirely (TTS+cloning+STT+MCP), VoiceStudio is actively developed vs 3.5 months cold, installs cleanly via uv+lockfile vs 5 dependency hacks, is strictly more capable (14 engines, dubbing, audiobooks, diarization), and doubling up costs a second multi-GB torch venv + a second claimant on the 48GB ledger for no new capability. Neither ships the dictation hotkey headless, so Voicebox adds nothing there either. **DEBI DECIDED 2026-08-07: AGPL OK (asked the right question — "does this block open-sourcing the harness?"; answer: NO, because a submodule/clone is a pointer not a copy, we run it as a SEPARATE PROCESS over HTTP/MCP (arm's-length ⇒ not a derivative work), and AGPL §13's network clause triggers only on MODIFIED versions — our never-edit-vendor doctrine prevents that. Red lines recorded: don't patch-and-serve to others, don't copy their source under our license, don't embed in a closed commercial product (they sell a license for that). Conveying it in the fat dmg is fine = unmodified source at a public tag + license included). BUILD BOTH, both OPTIONAL (`installed:false`), license on the card.**

best-effort `setuptools>=75,<80` (whisperx needs pkg_resources); bun SPA build if bun present else WARN+continue (backend serves a stub — graceful). voicebox = **exact mirror of upstream's `just setup-python`** read from source (pip; requirements.txt → `--no-deps chatterbox-tts` → `--no-deps hume-tada` → arm64: requirements-mlx.txt + `--no-deps mlx-audio==0.4.1` → `git+Qwen3-TTS`; linacodec/Zipvoice/piper-phonemize live INSIDE requirements.txt so pip handles them); bun root install + `build:web` then `cp -R web/dist → vendor/voicebox/frontend` (+ appended to `.git/info/exclude` so pin-bumps stay clean, with a `git ls-files` guard against clobbering if upstream ever tracks it). **start_component.sh**: both branches listener-scoped port-clear, argv arrays, cd-in-subshell w/ absolute pid+log, TRIES=150 `/health` poll, port read from harness.yaml. **TWO RECON CORRECTIONS THE CODE CAUGHT (agent read the real v0.5.0 source — exactly the discipline we want):** (1) **`VOICEBOX_DATA_DIR` DOES NOT EXIST** at that pin — `config.py` only reads `VOICEBOX_MODELS_DIR` and the data dir defaults to `Path("data")` **resolved against CWD**, so with `cd vendor/voicebox` it would have written its DB + generated audio INTO vendor/ → we now always pass `--data-dir "$ROOT/data/voicebox"`; (2) `backend/main.py` has NO `app = FastAPI()` — it re-exports `from .app import app`; the ASGI app, `/mcp` mount and frontend mount live in `backend/app.py` and `/health` in `backend/routes/health.py` → the contract test targets the REAL files (a single-file grep would have false-passed). Also confirmed: voicebox argparse `--port` really defaults to **8000**, so 17493 is ALWAYS passed explicitly. **bridge/app.py**: `_LOG_NAMES` + install plans (AGPL/no-auth warnings in the plan text = license visible pre-install) + install allowlist w/ **2h timeout** (`_script` gained a keyword `timeout`; TimeoutExpired now returns a readable message instead of a 500) + `_NOTES`; status/start/stop/start-plan were already generic over harness.yaml so the cards, plan→approve, and dependency closure came free. **panel**: both added to `LOG_SOURCES`. **app/main.swift**: 5 tabs (MC · Odysseus · Hermes · VoiceStudio · Voicebox), `vsWV`/`vbWV` plain WKWebViews (no skin, no DropWebView), lazy-loaded, shared delegates ⇒ the "Not reachable yet" placeholder + ⌘R retry work for free (and IS the normal state — these are usually stopped); **DropOverlay still panel-only (`idx != 0`), Hermes staleness auto-reload still pinned to idx 2**; structural validation only (no swiftc in sandbox) — braces 139/139, parens balanced string-aware, all idx 0-4 branches present. **Tests: contract 33 passed** (new test_voicestudio_contract + test_voicebox_contract skip CLEANLY until the repos exist, then pin app object / `/health` / `/mcp` / frontend mount / env names; `test_seam.py` component set updated — it asserts an exact set and would otherwise have failed), all 13 py + 6 js bridge suites green, CSS 488/488. ⚠️ **PENDING FABLE QA:** searxng-precedent cloning; uv-vs-pip split (voicestudio uv/lockfile, voicebox pip/layered --no-deps — mirrors each upstream); `setuptools<80` applied post-hoc best-effort (a later `uv sync` could prune it again); port single-sourced from yaml in start_component.sh but `_NOTES`/plan strings hardcode 3900/17493 (contract test asserts the port precisely so drift trips); > **🎙️ VOICE PHASE 3 SHIPPED — MCP fan-out + runner wiring (2026-08-07, Opus-5 builder, Fable-QA'd; Mac-verify PENDING):** the browse-toggle helper is now GENERALISED rather than copy-pasted: `_ody_mcp_list()`, `_hermes_config_path()`, `_hermes_write_mcp(name, entry|None)` (atomic temp+os.replace yaml round-trip for ANY server), with `_hermes_set_mcp` kept as a thin browsermcp wrapper (behaviour unchanged) — plus a new property: **a no-op toggle no longer rewrites `~/.hermes/config.yaml` at all**, so the single write that does happen is the only one that costs comments. New `VOICE_MCP` table + pure `voice_mcp_spec(name, port)` → `{url, odysseus_form (transport=http + url), hermes_entry ({"url":…}), tools, note}`. New **`GET /api/voice/status`** (per component: installed/running/odysseus/odysseus_known/hermes/connected/on/url/tools/port — `odysseus_known:false` distinguishes "Odysseus down" from "not registered") and **`POST /api/voice/toggle {name,on}`** — idempotent both ways, **re-registers a STALE url** (yaml port changed → delete+re-add rather than leave a silently-wrong entry), **409 on enable when not installed / nothing listening** (registering a dead url just makes Hermes log connection errors), returns `{ok, on, partial, odysseus, hermes, log}`. Panel: a **Voice tools (MCP)** group in **Capabilities → Tools** (NOT the composer — Fable compacted that today), built from the EXISTING `caps-group`/`cap-card`/`cap-row`/`cap-pill`/`cap-sw` grammar, **zero new CSS** (488/488 unchanged); disabled switch + `installed · stopped (:3900)` when down. **TWO MORE RECON CORRECTIONS THE CODE CAUGHT (web-verified at tag v0.4.2, not main):** (1) the client-id header is **`X-OmniVoice-Client-Id`**, NOT `X-VoiceStudio-Client-Id` (`backend/mcp_server.py::_current_client_id`) — and neither host can attach custom headers, so per-agent voice binding degrades to the global default voice (surfaced in the panel row, acceptable); (2) **`/mcp` only works because upstream sets `mcp.settings.streamable_http_path = "/"` before mounting** — without it the endpoint would be `/mcp/mcp`; now pinned by a contract test. **REAL BUG FOUND + FIXED:** VoiceStudio's in-process MCP tools call BACK into their own backend and default to `http://localhost:3900`, so a non-3900 port would have silently broken every MCP tool call → the start branch now exports `OMNIVOICE_API_URL=http://127.0.0.1:$VS_PORT`. **Runner wiring (VoiceStudio only; Voicebox bundles its own Qwen3):** verified env triplet `TRANSLATE_BASE_URL`/`TRANSLATE_MODEL`/`TRANSLATE_API_KEY` (the `custom` provider, `key_optional=True`) exported at start, reusing the **same wire-id rule as the hermes branch** (MLX needs the registry PATH, gguf the id); best-effort — no model ⇒ warn and skip, never blocks the start; deliberately did NOT set `LLM_DEFAULT_PROVIDER=custom` because at v0.4.2 a stored UI selection outranks the TRANSLATE_BASE_URL fallback, so our export makes the runner the default without stealing a choice Debi makes in VoiceStudio's own Settings. Tests: **contract 42 passed** (new `test_odysseus_mcp_register_form_contract` pins `@router.post("/servers")` + `@router.delete("/servers/{server_id}")` + the name/transport/url Form fields + `"url": srv.url` in the list response — which the stale-url check depends on; `test_hermes_mcp_url_entry_contract` pins `server_config["url"]` + streamable-http client), new **`bridge/tests/test_voice_mcp.py` 36/36** (spec builder + Hermes writer against a temp HERMES_HOME), all 14 py + 6 js suites green. ⚠️ **PENDING FABLE QA:** new `/api/voice/*` endpoints instead of extending `/api/browse/*` (browse is one hardcoded server, voice is a keyed table — browse's public shape untouched); `ok` = "at least one host reached the requested state" + `partial:true` ⇒ a user who never runs Odysseus sees `partial` on every voice toggle (honest but recurring); Hermes writes now skipped when unchanged (changes browse's always-rewrite behaviour); stale-url delete+re-add policy; 409 guard on enable only; TRANSLATE_* env is read at PROCESS START so a runner model switch needs a voicestudio restart to propagate; **Voicebox's tool names + FastMCP details are recon-reported, NOT tag-verified** (VoiceStudio's are) — the contract tests assert them the moment the repo exists; the voice server also appears in "Connected tools (MCP)" above once enabled (it IS a real Odysseus server — noted in the group description rather than filtered); `OMNIVOICE_MCP_ALLOWED_HOSTS` left unset (SDK default localhost-only, correct for loopback). **🔧 NO-BREW SELF-PROVISIONING (2026-08-07, Debi's ask: "the finished app should install this itself, not make the user run brew first" — correct, and auto-brew is a REJECTED design verdict + the HN-pitfall audit promises we never write outside our own tree):** new `scripts/ensure_bun.sh` + `scripts/ensure_ffmpeg.sh`, both idempotent, both PRINT-PATH-on-stdout/prose-to-stderr, both NON-FATAL to callers (`set -e` safe — verified by simulation). Resolution order for each: (a) the user's own on PATH (never shadowed), (b) our cached `data/bun/bin/bun` / `data/ffmpeg/bin/ffmpeg` (bun re-fetched if `--version` drifts from the pin, so pin-bumps actually take), (c) provision. bun = pinned GitHub release zip (`build.bun_pin: bun-v1.3.14` — release-asset presence VERIFIED before pinning, the exact llama.cpp CI-lag trap) unzipped into `data/bun/`, layout-agnostic find+relocate mirroring install_llamacpp.sh; `BUN_INSTALL`/`BUN_INSTALL_CACHE_DIR` forced into `data/bun/` even when using the user's own bun (absolute no-writes-outside rule, at the cost of not reusing `~/.bun` cache). ffmpeg = the **`imageio-ffmpeg` wheel** (`build.imageio_ffmpeg_pin: 0.6.0`, BSD-2, already a VoiceStudio dep) installed into the CALLING component's venv, its bundled static binary COPIED (not symlinked — a rebuilt venv can't dangle it) to `data/ffmpeg/bin/`; chosen over a static-build download so there's no third-party binary host to trust and no URL to pin+bump; only pip-installs when `import imageio_ffmpeg` fails so it can't perturb voicestudio's `uv sync --frozen` env. start_component.sh PATH-prepends our ffmpeg dir at runtime when it isn't the system one. Install plan text in bridge/app.py updated to tell the truth ("will download bun into data/ if not present"), AGPL/no-auth/fragile warnings intact. **HONEST LIMIT: imageio-ffmpeg bundles `ffmpeg` ONLY — there is no `ffprobe`** (verified: one file in the wheel's binaries/); any upstream path shelling out to ffprobe specifically still needs a real install. FAT installer UNTOUCHED and cannot start requiring either (helpers are called only from the two online-only optional branches; build_app.sh/firstrun_fat.sh contain zero references). Paths written, all inside the repo tree: `data/bun/{bin,cache}`, `data/ffmpeg/bin/ffmpeg`, existing venvs, vendor SPA outputs. No brew, no sudo, no `~/.bun`. ⚠️ PENDING FABLE QA: ffmpeg extended to voicestudio too (brief said voicebox only — whisperx/demucs shell out to ffmpeg by name); pin read moved AFTER the PATH/cache branches (a user with their own bun must not be blocked by a missing key); `bash -c 'bun install && bun run build'` in an env-prefixed subshell so package.json scripts resolving `bun`/`bunx` by name work; copy-not-symlink ≈50MB in data/. **✅ BOTH VOICE COMPONENTS MAC-VERIFIED (Debi, 2026-08-07): 6/6 installed, 6/6 running.** Voicebox needed FOUR fixes before its venv would build, all from real errors, none of them dependency-related: (1) interpreter preference → the harness's own bundled standalone CPython (`data/python-standalone/bin/python3`) ahead of a broken/relinked Homebrew python3.12 whose `ensurepip` aborted venv creation outright; (2) venv-creation fallback chain `uv venv --seed` → `python -m venv` → `python -m venv --without-pip`; (3) **`uv` resolved EXPLICITLY by path** (`command -v uv`, `~/.local/bin/uv`, `/opt/homebrew/bin/uv`, `/usr/local/bin/uv`, `~/.cargo/bin/uv`) — a Finder-launched app gets a MINIMAL PATH, so `command -v uv` missed a uv in `~/.local/bin` even though the same shell found `/opt/homebrew/bin/bun`; that single miss was why both pip-bootstrap routes were silently unavailable; (4) a pip-EXISTS guard after venv creation with three bootstrap routes — ensurepip → uv → **the FAT app's bundled wheelhouse via the pip-wheel zipapp trick** (`python <wheelhouse>/pip-*.whl/pip install --no-index --find-links <wheelhouse> pip setuptools wheel`, needs no network and no extra tooling; sandbox-verified working), because a uv-seeded / `--without-pip` venv can have the pip MODULE without the `bin/pip` SCRIPT — the literal `bin/pip: No such file or directory` we hit. All pip calls now go through `python -m pip`, never `bin/pip`. **DISK: retries do NOT accumulate** (fixed paths, overwrite-in-place, venv `rm -rf`'d on failure, log truncated per run) — measured 104.9→101.1 GB free across the whole exercise ≈ 3.8GB for both components. **⚠️ PENDING FABLE QA: the LESSON is the general one — any script the BRIDGE spawns runs with a Finder-minimal PATH, so every external tool must be resolved by explicit path list, not `command -v` alone.** ensure_bun.sh/ensure_ffmpeg.sh already do this; anything future must too.

Its SPA was built with **Debi's own `/opt/homebrew/bin/bun`** ⇒ the "reuse yours, download nothing" path is CONFIRMED working; `installed: true` flip landed correctly in the snapshot manifest. **VOICEBOX FAILED AT VENV CREATION** (not deps, not bun): `python3.12 -m venv` aborted with `ensurepip --upgrade --default-pip returned non-zero exit status 1` — a broken/relinked Homebrew python, not a harness bug; `installed:false` stayed correct (nothing half-installed). FIX SHIPPED: the voicebox branch now (a) PREFERS the harness's own bundled standalone CPython (`data/python-standalone/bin/python3` — 3.12.x, exactly upstream's preferred minor, and the interpreter that already built every other venv here, so its ensurepip is known-good) before falling back to system python3.12/3.13/3, echoing which one it picked; and (b) wraps venv creation in a fallback chain — `python -m venv` → `uv venv --python <PY> --seed` → `python -m venv --without-pip` + `ensurepip --upgrade` → loud failure naming the log and the two repair options. Both branches simulation-tested. Also confirmed by this run: the `[ship] manifest:` merge works, the install plan dialog renders the license + no-auth warnings before anything runs, and a tab that was opened BEFORE its component started keeps the "Not reachable yet" placeholder until ⌘R (expected, documented).

> **✅ FABLE 5 QA OF THE OPUS-5 SESSION — COMPLETE (2026-08-06):** reviewed every ⚠️ from the 2026-08-06 Opus-5 substitute run. **VERDICTS — approved as built:** path-guard fence (rule_key=containing-dir grain, description line on the card, audit-tier bridge-cwd over-flag, mini-yaml fallback parser); repetition guard values (1.1/256 — mild, standard; note: applies to ALL gguf models, tunable later); MLX wire-id = registry path (Hermes dashboard showing the path = accepted cost; revisit if mlx-lm ever ships an alias flag); MTP detection substring match id-OR-repo (false-positive risk negligible + self-healing retry contains it) + spec_mtp override semantics; llama.cpp b10295 pin discipline; mlx pin single-sourcing (build.mlx_*_pin). **THREE DEFECTS FOUND + FIXED by Fable:** (1) thinking rehydration DROPPED reasoning-only assistant turns — upstream's own projection deliberately KEEPS them (#44022, server.py _history_to_messages) so extended-thinking turns don't vanish on reopen → mapper now keeps them (panel already handles content:'' + disclosure); (2) mapper's key set had two nonexistent names and MISSED upstream's structured keys → now exactly reasoning/reasoning_content/reasoning_details/codex_reasoning_items with defensive list-flattening (surprise types can never break a transcript); (3) chat-header live-model fix trusted `runner.pin` even when STOPPED (pin falls back to intent model → a dead runner would lie) → now `running`-gated, shows 'no model loaded' when stopped; plus the `stampFor(s && s.model)` undefined-arg edge (undefined = re-stamp sentinel) fixed at the caller. test_hermes_sessions extended 18→23 checks (reasoning-only kept, flatten, precedence, surprise types); all suites + contract 12/12 green.

> **🚀 MTP ACCELERATION SHIPPED + MEASURED (2026-08-06, Opus 5 builder-mode; ⚠️ PENDING FABLE QA):** MTP now actually engages and is **+15% avg** (controlled A/B, same model Qwen3.5-9B-Q4_0, same binary b10295, same prompt, only the flags differ): OFF = 45.83 tok/s (σ±0.32, n=4, FLAT across 723→3178 tok) vs ON = 56.43 / 52.05 / 49.57 → the gain DECAYS with generation length (+22% short → +8% at 2.5k tok; expected as attention cost rises + draft acceptance falls). Lowest ON sample still beats the highest OFF sample by 8% ⇒ outside noise. **THE FIX:** detection now matches the registry id **OR the source repo** (`_gguf_registry_entry`/`_mlx_registry_entry` persist `repo`; MTP models ship as `<org>/…-MTP-GGUF` while the per-file id has no marker — that gap was the whole reason MTP loaded but never accelerated), plus `runner.spec_mtp: auto|on|off` override and a **self-healing retry**: if a spec launch fails, start_component.sh warns, strips SPEC_ARGS and relaunches clean (so broadening detection can't strand the runner). New `bridge/tests/test_mtp_detect.py` pins repo-persistence + the conservative decision table (no MTP evidence ⇒ NEVER add flags). **llama.cpp pin b10094 → b10295** (b10297 existed but had NO macos-arm64 asset — CI lags a fresh tag; verified `--spec-type`/`--repeat-penalty`/`--alias` all present). **MLX needed no upgrade** (PyPI latest = installed: mlx-lm 0.31.3 / mlx-vlm 0.6.10 / mlx 0.32.0) so install_mlx.sh now PINS those versions instead of floating. Old binary kept at `data/llamacpp.b10094.bak` for rollback.

Consider showing the live model, or both. (2) On reopening a session, thinking/INSPECT don't render: for the direct lane that's correct (we persist `{role,content}` only), but for the **Hermes lane the reasoning IS in Hermes's store** (`{role,text,reasoning*}`) and our Phase-3 mapper drops reasoning-only turns — so a collapsed "thinking" disclosure COULD be rehydrated for Hermes sessions. Fable's call.

> **✅ MTP CHECKLIST CLOSED (Debi, 2026-08-06) + broken model deleted:** a **GGUF** MTP model (`Qwen3.5-9B-Q4_0` from `unsloth/Qwen3.5-9B-MTP-GGUF`) downloaded via OUR download manager, went LIVE, and chats coherently → MTP loads/serves fine, no harness bug. The broken `oQ4e-mtp-XL-mlx` model was deleted. ⚠️ **HONEST CAVEAT: the speculative-decoding flags did NOT engage** — the gate is `[[ "$R_MODEL" =~ [Mm][Tt][Pp] ]]` on the registry **id** (`Qwen3.5-9B-Q4_0`), but "MTP" only appears in the SOURCE REPO name, and `_gguf_registry_entry` doesn't persist the repo. So we have MTP working, not MTP-accelerated. **OPTIONAL perf slice (Debi's call, not a bug):** persist `repo` on the download registry entry + broaden the MTP detection to id OR repo, and measure tok/s before/after (detection must stay conservative — spec flags on a non-MTP model break loading).

> **✅ MLX WIRE-ID BUG FIXED + VERIFIED (Opus 5 builder-mode, 2026-08-06) — found by the MTP test:** MLX chat returned `[proxy error: runner 400]` because `mlx_lm.server` treats the request's `model` field as A MODEL TO LOAD (ModelProvider.load → mlx_lm.utils.load → HF resolve); we sent our REGISTRY ID → 404 on huggingface.co → 400. llama.cpp was immune only because we launch it with `--alias <registry id>`. **NO alias flag exists in mlx-lm 0.31.3 or mlx-vlm 0.6.10** (argv read in-sandbox from the real packages) → FIX = new pure `wire_model_id()`/`display_model_id()` in bridge/app.py: gguf → registry id (unchanged), **mlx → the registry `path`, byte-identical to the `--model` argv** so mlx-lm resolves the ALREADY-LOADED model (no 21GB reload). Applied at every wire site: direct lane payload, Hermes config fan-out (start_component.sh, now YAML-QUOTED — a path with a space would corrupt ~/.hermes/config.yaml), Odysseus seed (`seed_odysseus_jan.py` resolves it itself + `HARNESS_WIRE_MODEL` override). Registry id stays the internal key everywhere (ledger/labels/selects); 4 bridge endpoints map back for display. **ROOT CAUSE of the bogus `K2-FSA/OMNIVOICE` header ALSO found:** mlx_lm.server's `/v1/models` enumerates the shared **HuggingFace cache** (`scan_cache_dir()`, loaded model appended LAST) — Debi had installed OmniVoice via another app — and `seed_odysseus_jan._discover_model` was taking `data[0].id` as Odysseus's default_model. MLX probe ids are never trusted now. Tests: test_load_switch **36/36** (19 new wire/display cases), all suites + contract 12/12 green. VERIFIED on Mac: 400 gone, header/model_info correct.
> **⛔ MTP-MLX MODEL ITSELF IS BROKEN — MTP verify BLOCKED (2026-08-06):** `Qwen3.6-35B-A3B-oQ4e-mtp-XL-mlx` emits token soup (`<|im_start|>` leaking, multilingual garbage) at temp 0 under BOTH our mlx-lm AND **LM Studio's own mlx-engine** → two independent engines, identical failure ⇒ the `oQ4e` MTP-XL quant is mis-quantized/experimental, NOT a harness or mlx-lm bug. MLX does support MTP in general. To close the MTP checklist item, test a **known-good MTP model** — preferably a GGUF MTP (the `--spec-type draft-mtp` flags already exist in start_component.sh's llamacpp branch, gated on binary support). Debi may delete this model. ⚠️ **ALL design calls in the 2026-08-06 session were made by OPUS 5 in builder/substitute mode, NOT Fable** (path-guard spec, mlx wire-id decision, repetition-penalty values) — the identity protocol sanctions only Opus 4.8 as a substitute and predates Opus 5: treat these as PENDING FABLE QA.

> **🔍 WS-1005 CARRY-OVER CLOSED — VERDICT: INDEPENDENT, NOT BRIDGE-CAUSED (Fable, 2026-08-06, recon-evidenced):** the dashboard's "events feed disconnected" banner + "[session ended (code 1005)]" come from the BROWSER side — WebKit tearing down a backgrounded WKWebView tab's sockets. Evidence: the banner's feed is `/api/events` (web_server.py:15633, fed only by the PTY child's `/api/pub` mirror — plumbing the bridge never touches); both `/api/events` and `/api/ws` are true fan-out (subscriber SETS, per-session transport routing server.py:1338-1389); every server-side close carries an explicit code (4400-4410/1011/1000) so 1005 = closed-without-status = local teardown; loopback runs NO WS pings (web_server.py:17212) so the drop is only noticed at close; upstream's ChatPage misclassifies a benign 1005 as terminal ("ended") and refuses auto-reconnect (pty-reconnect.ts:56-58) — an UPSTREAM client bug. REMEDY: ⌘R the Hermes tab (or the banner's reconnect button). Optional later polish: auto-reload the Hermes webview on tab re-select when stale (main.swift). **KNOWN LIMIT recorded: don't drive the SAME Hermes session from panel + dashboard simultaneously** — prompt.submit/session.resume rebind the session's transport to the caller (methods_prompt.py:126-131), silently muting the other surface's view. Also noted: bridge turns share the active-session cap pool if `max_concurrent_sessions` is ever set (unset today). Optional live confirm: background the Hermes tab ~15min with the bridge idle → banner appears anyway → confirmed WebKit.

Carry-over #2 CLOSED. Remaining: WS-1005 investigation (carry-over #1), MTP load check, PAT revoke.
